# Part 1 — Statistik untergebrachter wohnungsloser Personen (Destatis, EVAS 22971)

Ingestor: `ingest/domains/counts_law.py` (part 1) · researched and run 2026-11-20 (Europe/Berlin).

## What the statistic is
- Federal statistic under the **Wohnungslosenberichterstattungsgesetz (WoBerichtsG, 4 Mar 2020)**, run by
  Destatis every year with **Stichtag 31 January** (the night 31 Jan → 1 Feb). First wave 2022, so the
  series is 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026.
- It counts **only people who are housed because they are homeless**: placed by municipalities under police
  and public-order law (ordnungsrechtlich), placed under SGB XII §§ 67 ff. / in "Betreutes Wohnen" of freie Träger,
  or housed through other municipal funding (e.g. Kältehilfe). Since 2023 it also includes recognised refugees
  and Ukrainians (§ 24 AufenthG) who are still housed because they have no tenancy.
- **Not counted:** people sleeping rough ("ohne Unterkunft"), people staying with friends or family ("verdeckt
  wohnungslos"), residents of care homes, women's shelters, addiction clinics and youth-welfare housing, people
  who only use counselling services, and people at risk of eviction. The **BMWSB Wohnungslosenbericht** (every
  two years: 2022, 2024, next due ~2026) estimates the uncounted groups. Keep the two series separate in the
  atlas, and never add them together without that report's own method.
- Sources: the Destatis theme page https://www.destatis.de/DE/Themen/Gesellschaft-Umwelt/Soziales/Wohnungslosigkeit/_inhalt.html,
  the quality report https://www.destatis.de/DE/Methoden/Qualitaet/Qualitaetsberichte/Soziales/wohnungslose-personen.pdf,
  and WISTA 1/2023 https://www.destatis.de/DE/Methoden/WISTA-Wirtschaft-und-Statistik/2023/01/neue-statistik-untergebrachter-wohnungsloser-personen-012023.pdf

### How the data is fetched (and the access blocker)
- The **classic GENESIS webservice** (`https://www-genesis.destatis.de/genesisWS/rest/2020/`, now redirecting to
  `genesis.destatis.de/genesisWS/rest/2020/`) accepts `GAST/GAST` for `helloworld/logincheck`. It refuses
  `data/tablefile`, `catalogue/*` and `find` results with **Code 15 "nicht berechtigt"**. The same happens on
  regionalstatistik.de. A free registered GENESIS account would unlock it; none was used (no sign-ups).
- The public GENESIS-Online web table view renders every table anonymously from plain GETs. The
  ingestor reads those same JSON resources, with no cookies, session or token:
  `https://genesis.destatis.de/genesis/api/rest/tables/<code>/data` (JSON-stat-like cube: `id`, `size`,
  `dimension`, `value`, `status`) and `.../structure` (labels, `lastChange`). This is undocumented and could
  change. The fallback is a registered webservice account, or the "Download" button on the table page.
- Raw responses are cached in `data/raw/counts_law/` (11 files, 2.4 MB: 3 data cubes, 3 structures, 5 press releases). Every fetch is logged to
  `logs/ingest-counts_law.jsonl`.

## Fail-loud guard on the GENESIS fetch (added at the Lead's request, 2026-11-21)
The anonymous JSON endpoint has no documentation, so `validate_cube()` runs on every cube before anything is
written and raises `GenesisShapeError` with a clear message when:
- the response has no `data` list, or the cube lacks `id` / `size` / `dimension` / `value`;
- a required dimension is missing or renamed (`STAG`, `NAT`, `GES02`, `ALT026`, plus the region variable);
- a category index length does not match `size`, or `len(value)` / `len(status)` does not equal the product of the sizes;
- the **Stichtag set differs from `EXPECTED_STAG`** (= one 31 January per entry in `PRESS`). A new year therefore
  stops the ingest until someone adds its press-release URL to `PRESS`, which is intended;
- the NAT / sex / age category codes change;
- there are **zero non-empty cells**; fewer than 16 Länder or 390 Kreise carry values; the Land codes differ from 01–16;
- `ingest_table` would return 0 metric rows;
- a press release cannot be fetched, its total no longer matches the regex, or the **national total differs from the
  press release by more than 1,000** (`check_press`).

Before this change, press-check failures were only noted. Now they abort the run.

**Test.** `python3 -m ingest.domains.counts_law --shape-test` runs offline against the cached cubes. It also runs as
the first step of `--selftest`. It feeds 10 malformed copies of the 22971-0001/-0050 cubes to the guard: missing
`value`, missing `STAG`, renamed `ALT026`, a 30 June Stichtag, a dropped Stichtag, truncated values, all cells empty,
empty `data`, a changed sex code, and one Land with no values. It also runs `ingest_table` end-to-end on an all-empty
cube with `conn=None`, which proves the raise happens before any DB access, plus 3 bad `check_press` inputs.
**All 14 raise**; the real cubes and the real press totals pass.

## What lands in the DB
- `pit`: `geo_ags` = `DE` / Land AGS (2-digit) / Kreis AGS (5-digit), `geo_kind` = `bund` / `land` / `kreis`, `year` = Stichtag
  year, **`sheltered`** = housed homeless persons, **`children`** = persons under 18, `is_est=0`. `total` and `unsheltered` are
  left NULL **on purpose**. This statistic counts only people who are housed, so writing it as a total would overstate
  coverage. The Lead should decide whether the site reads `sheltered` when `total` is NULL. Rows: bund 5, land 80, kreis 2,000.
- `metric`: `entity_type` = `de` (`DE`) / `land` (AGS2) / `kreis` (AGS5), unit `persons`, `is_est=0`. Metric names:
  `housed_homeless`, `housed_homeless__{male,female,sex_unknown}`, `housed_homeless__{german,foreign}`,
  `housed_homeless__{age_lt18,age_18_24,age_25_39,age_40_59,age_60plus,age_unknown}`. `note` carries the Stichtag,
  the table code, the GENESIS status flag, the human table URL and the table's `lastChange`. Rows: 25,020.
- `geo_land` (16) and `geo_kreis` (400): **INSERT OR IGNORE** only (ags, name, abbr / land_ags, land, kind
  `landkreis|kreisfreie_stadt`). Rows another domain (BKG VG250) already created are never modified.
- `counts_law_destatis_cell`: every published cell of the three cubes (175,140 rows), including the full
  nationality × sex × age cross-tab and the GENESIS status flag.
- `source`: 3 GENESIS data URLs, 3 structure URLs, 5 press releases (11 rows).
- Press cross-check (runs inside the module): the rounded headline total of every press release agrees with
  22971-0001 within ±1,000. Result: `2022:178000~178145 2023:372000~372060 2024:439500~439465 2025:474700~474675
  2026:452900~452910`, all ok.

Selftest in a scratch DB (`--db ~/projects/_staging/scratch/counts_law.db`, never `data/atlas.db`) gave rows=27,521
(metric 25,020 + pit 2,085 + geo 416). A second run produced identical table counts (idempotent; geo inserts 0 the second time).

## Caveats
1. **Housed persons only.** This is not "homelessness in Germany". Rough sleepers and hidden homeless people are missing (see above).
2. **The 2022→2023 and 2023→2024 jumps are mostly reporting artefacts.** Destatis attributes the rises largely to
   better reporting in the 2nd, 3rd and 4th waves, plus roughly 130,000 Ukrainians first counted in 2023 (PD23_305; PD24_282;
   PD25_246). Do not present 2022→2025 as a trend in homelessness. 2026 is the first decline (−5 %).
3. **Rounding to multiples of 5:** every published cell is a multiple of 5 (confidentiality). Land and Kreis sums
   differ from the DE total by up to ±55 persons in 2022–2026. The national figure is always read from 22971-0001, never summed.
4. **Status `-` = 0.** GENESIS marks "nothing reported" with `-`. These are stored as 0. Between 4 and 9 Kreise per
   year have total = 0 (2022: 8, 2023: 8, 2024: 9, 2025: 6, 2026: 4). This may be under-reporting rather than true zero.
5. **Reporting is by the reporting body's location,** not the person's origin. City-states and large cities dominate.
   Kreisfreie Städte and Landkreise cannot be compared without population (VG250 / Destatis 12411).
6. **Revisions:** table `lastChange` was 2026-11-20 (UTC) at fetch time, so GENESIS revises published cells. Use `--refresh`
   to re-download. Each metric's `note` records the `lastChange` that was seen.
7. **Pre-reform Kreis codes** (e.g. `05313 Aachen, kreisfreie Stadt (bis 20.10.2009)`) are in the classification but
   carry no data, and are skipped. 400 current Kreise are kept.
8. Nationality "unknown/stateless" is not a separate value in these tables (DE total − German − foreign is
   about 3–5 % per press releases). It is not derived here.

## Kreis codes in 22971-0080: 16056, 09475, 16062, 06415 (Lead request 2026-11-21)
Evidence comes from the table's own structure (`/tables/22971-0080/structure`, `variableValues.KREISE` labels) and
data (`/tables/22971-0080/data`, status per cell), both archived in `data/raw/counts_law/` (sha1 cache). The module
also archives and verifies four documents (`KREIS_EVIDENCE`, files `kreis_<key>.*`). The run fails loudly if a
phrase is missing.

The cube lists **477 KREISE codes**. 76 carry a historic label, "(bis dd.mm.yyyy)" or "(b.dd.mm.yyyy)". The other
401 are current: the 400 Kreise of 2021–2025 plus 06415 Hanau from 2026. Cell status is either `e` (value) or `-`;
there are no `.` (secret) cells. Every 0 is published as `-` (114,645 cells), and every `e` cell is non-zero
(85,695).

| code | label in the structure | what the data shows (total, Stichtag 31.01.) | handling in the module |
|---|---|---|---|
| **16056** | "Eisenach, kreisfreie Stadt (bis 30.06.2021)" | 2022: **10** (`e`); 2023–2026: `-` | **Folded into 16063 Wartburgkreis.** TLS records "01.07.2021 · 56000 Eisenach, Stadt · Eingliederung in einen Landkreis (Status der kreisfreien Stadt wird aufgegeben) · 63105 Eisenach, Stadt" (`tls_eisenach`). Destatis shows results "auf Grundlage des Ortes der Unterbringung der wohnungslosen Personen" (`destatis_22971_info`). So the 10 persons Destatis still filed under the dissolved key on 31.01.2022 were accommodated in what was then already Wartburgkreis territory. metric/pit 16063 for 2022 = 10 (published for 16063) + 10 (published for 16056) = **20**, for every margin with a value. The note names both cells, and the sum of two 5-rounded cells can be off by up to 4. The raw 16056 cells stay as published in `counts_law_destatis_cell`. No geo_kreis, metric or pit row is kept for 16056; rows an earlier version wrote are deleted (own rows only). 16063 for 2023–2026 is unchanged. |
| **09475** | "Hof, Landkreis" | `-` on all 5 Stichtage, every margin | Kept as a current Kreis with **0** on every Stichtag. The note reads "published as '-' (nichts vorhanden) on every Stichtag 2022-2026". |
| **16062** | "Nordhausen, Landkreis" | `-` on all 5 Stichtage, every margin | same as 09475 |
| 06415 | "Hanau, kreisfreie Stadt" | 2022–2025 `-`, 2026: 920 | Hanau became kreisfrei on 01.01.2026; before that it was part of 06435 Main-Kinzig-Kreis. Source: "Die Stadt Hanau wurde mit Wirkung zum 1. Januar 2026 … zur kreisfreien Stadt erklärt und aus dem Main-Kinzig-Kreis ausgegliedert" (Hessisches Ministerium des Innern, `he_hanau`). The 2022–2025 `-` therefore means the unit did not exist yet, so no metric or pit rows are written for those years. Raw cells are kept. |

Why Hof and Nordhausen were missing before: the previous code kept only Kreis codes with at least one `e` value. That
dropped the two current Kreise that have only `-`, and kept the dissolved 16056 because of its single 2022 value.

**Are Hof and Nordhausen suppressed, missing or folded?**
- **Not suppressed.** The statistic uses only the 5er-Rundung as its secrecy procedure: "Zur Sicherstellung der
  Geheimhaltung erfolgt die Veröffentlichung … unter Einsatz des Geheimhaltungsverfahrens der 5er-Rundung … Die
  maximale Abweichung zu den jeweiligen Originalwerten beträgt dadurch für jeden Wert höchstens 2"
  (`destatis_22971_info`, section 1.8.2). The cube has no `.` cells ("Zahlenwert unbekannt oder geheim zu halten").
- **Published as "nichts vorhanden".** In the Destatis legend `–` means "nichts vorhanden" (`destatis_zeichen`,
  WISTA 4/2026, Zeichenerklärung). Because of the 5er-Rundung, a published 0 can also stand for a true count of 1
  or 2. The table does not separate these cases.
- **Not folded, according to the metadata.** Neither label carries a date or merger note. Both codes are current
  Kreise, and 09464 Hof (kreisfreie Stadt) and 16063/16064/16065 are reported separately. Assignment is by place of
  accommodation, not by the seat of the reporting authority. Nothing in GENESIS says that Landkreis Hof's or
  Nordhausen's people are counted elsewhere. GENESIS also cannot show whether the reporting bodies in these two
  Kreise reported no one, or whether people placed by them were accommodated in another Kreis (for example the city
  of Hof). The LfStat Bayern or the TLS could confirm this; they were not contacted (contact rule).
- **Site wording:** show 0 with the footnote "von Destatis als '–' (nichts vorhanden) veröffentlicht, 5er-gerundet".
  Do not show it as "no data".

Kreis rows per Stichtag after the fix: 2022–2025 **400**, 2026 **401**. Kreis sums (2022: 178,125 vs DE 178,145)
stay within the rounding tolerance.

**Hanau 06415 and the population gap (checked 2026-11-23 against lead/core data/atlas.db, read-only).** 22971-0080
prints Hanau as its own Kreis code 06415 only for Stichtag 31.01.2026 (920 persons; 2022–2025 "-"), and the
Main-Kinzig-Kreis 06435 drops from 2,985 (2025) to 1,700 (2026) — 1,700 + 920 = 2,620, i.e. the split is in the
data. geo_kreis already holds 06415 as kind `kreisfreie_stadt` with geometry, and geo_gemeinde 06415000 has
einwohner 98,582 (GV-ISys 30.09.2026, Bevölkerung 31.12.2025), but no Kreis-level `einwohner` metric exists for 06415
(06435 has 2021–2025 from geo_bevoelkerung, none for 2026). Recommendation: keep the 06415 rows at Kreis level (that
is how Destatis publishes them and how every other kreisfreie Stadt is stored); the missing population is a
geo_bevoelkerung (Lead) gap, not ours: fill Kreis 06415 from the Gemeinde 06415000 figure (or Regionaldatenbank once
it publishes the 2026 Kreis codes), and note that a 2026 per-capita rate for 06435 needs the Main-Kinzig population
without Hanau.

## Coverage
- **Counts:** 100 % of the published 22971 universe at DE, Land and Kreis level for all five Stichtage (2022–2026),
  with sex, nationality and age margins plus the full cross-tab. 400/400 current Kreise, 16/16 Länder.
- **Missing in part 1:** the household-type, household-size, provider, type-of-provision and duration tables (-0002…-0008,
  -0051…-0055), each cheap to add with the same decoder. There is no Gemeinde-level table. Population denominators
  (per-10k rates) await VG250 / population. The BMWSB estimates of unhoused and hidden homeless people belong in a separate module.

# Part 2 — Estimates: BMWSB Wohnungslosenbericht and BAG W Hochrechnung

## Sources (archived as `data/raw/counts_law/<key>.pdf` plus the sha1 cache copy)
| key | document | URL | bytes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `wb2024` | Wohnungslosenbericht der Bundesregierung 2024 (BMWSB; GISS/Verian survey 1–7 Feb 2024), 121 pp. | https://www.bmwsb.bund.de/SharedDocs/downloads/DE/veroeffentlichungen/wohnen/wohnungslosenbericht-2024.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=1 | 4,828,419 |
| `wb2022` | Ausmaß und Struktur von Wohnungslosigkeit: Der Wohnungslosenbericht 2022 (BMAS; GISS/Kantar survey 1–7 Feb 2022), 80 pp. | https://www.bmwsb.bund.de/SharedDocs/downloads/DE/veroeffentlichungen/wohnen/wohnungslosenbericht-2022.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2 | 1,392,190 |
| `bagw2025` | BAG W Pressemappe, 17 Nov 2025 (Hochrechnung 2023/2024) | https://www.bagw.de/fileadmin/bagw/media/Doc/PRM/PRM_25_BAG_W_Hochrechnung_2025_Pressemappe.pdf | 1,676,632 |
| `bagw2023` | BAG W Pressemappe, 7 Nov 2023 (Hochrechnung 2021/2022) | https://www.bagw.de/fileadmin/bagw/media/Doc/PRM/PRM_PM_BAG_W_Pressemappe_Hochrechnung_Zahl_der_wohnungslosen_Menschen.pdf | 1,988,861 |
| `bagw2021` | BAG W Pressemitteilung, 21 Dec 2021 (Schätzung 2018–2020) | https://www.bagw.de/fileadmin/bagw/media/Doc/PRM/PRM_2021_12_21_BAGW_Schaetzung.pdf | 593,009 |

**No newer Wohnungslosenbericht exists yet.** The 2026 survey (Difu/RegioKontext, 1–7 Feb 2026, 300 municipalities) is due for
publication by the BMWSB at the end of 2026 (https://www.wohnungslosenberichterstattung2026.de/z%C3%A4hlung-und-befragung). The
newest BAG W Hochrechnung is the Nov 2025 release; nothing newer appears on https://www.bagw.de/de/themen/zahl-der-wohnungslosen/uebersicht.

## How numbers are guaranteed to be in the document
`FIGURES` in `counts_law.py` lists each figure with its document, its **PDF page** (1-based page index from `pdftotext -layout`)
and its printed form (German separator, e.g. `47.270`, or a longer phrase where a number repeats). Before inserting, the ingestor
runs `pdftotext` on the archived PDF and checks that this string appears on that page. Figures that fail are skipped and listed
in `notes`. Selftest result: **110 figures written, 0 skipped**. For the 2024 report the PDF page equals the printed page. For the
2022 report, PDF page = printed page + 2.

## What lands in the DB (`metric`, unit `persons`)
- `entity_type='de'`, `entity_id='DE'`; `land` (AGS2) for single Länder; `land_group` with `entity_id` = AGS joined by `+`
  (`01+03+04`, `06+07+10`, `12+13+14+15+16`) for the report's grouped Länder. These ids are not AGS codes and are never written to geo tables.
  `de_gkpol` (`GKPOL1-5`, `GKPOL6`, `GKPOL7`) holds the municipality-size classes.
- **is_est=1** on every survey extrapolation (Hochrechnung/Schätzung). The only `is_est=0` rows are `wb_housed` and
  `wb_housed__minors`: the Destatis count as reprinted in each report. They are kept separate from the GENESIS rows, and
  2024 prints 439.466, one more than GENESIS 439,465.
- Nothing goes to `pit`. The selftest asserts that every `pit` row cites a genesis.destatis.de URL.
- Row counts: 110 estimate rows (BAG W 29; WB: de 48 (45 estimates + 3 reprinted Destatis counts), de_gkpol 9, land 15, land_group 9).
  Total metric rows = 25,130 (25,020 Destatis + 110). A repeat run gives identical counts.

## Caveats (part 2)
1. **Different populations and reference periods.** Destatis = housed persons on the night of 31 Jan. WB = unsheltered and
   hidden homeless people with contact to help services in the week 1–7 Feb. BAG W = BAG W Wohnungsnotfall definition, counted over
   the calendar year or on 30 June, extrapolated from NRW's Wohnungsnotfallberichterstattung. Never add BAG W to WB/Destatis figures.
2. **The 2022 and 2024 WB `wb_unsheltered`/`wb_hidden` figures differ in definition.** 2024 includes minors (2,051 / 6,602), while 2022
   excludes the 1,100 / 5,500 minors living with parents. Compare like for like through `*_surveyed` or `*__adults`.
   The report calls the 2022 count an undercount (COVID closures; p. 23 of wb2024).
3. **Land figures exist only for 2024 and only in groups.** Five single Länder (HH, NW, BW, BY, BE) and three groups.
   The figures exclude minors living with parents.
4. Tab. 3-4 sex/age counts cover only respondents who gave both answers (42,394 of 45,338 unsheltered; 52,270 of 53,869 hidden).
5. BAG W changed method between the 2021 release (help system + recognised refugees) and the 2023/2025 releases. The 2019 release
   gives 542,000 for 30.06.2018, while the 2021 table gives 543,000; the latest table is used.
6. The WB reprints Destatis 2024 as 439,466 (GENESIS today: 439,465) and 2022 as 178,100 (rounded). Both are stored under
   `wb_housed`, separate from the Destatis rows, so nothing is overwritten.

## Verified (part 2)
The automatic check above confirms that every stored figure is present on its page. In addition, I read the pages by hand:
wb2024 p. 7 (531.600 / 439.500 / 60.400 / 47.300 / 15.600 rounded), p. 22 Tab. 3-1, p. 24 Tab. 3-3, p. 86 Tab. 5-1;
wb2022 PDF p. 57 Tab. 5.1.1; bagw2025 p. 1; bagw2021 p. 5.

## Coverage (part 2)
Federal WB headline, group, minor, overlap, GKPOL, Land-group and sex/age counts for both editions (2022 printed only rounded
headline counts; its structure tables are percentages only and are not stored). BAG W: all national annual, 30-June and rough-sleeper
figures 2018–2024 and the 2024 breakdowns. Not stored: percentage-only tables (household type, nationality shares, health,
violence, discrimination), which are share data without a count; they could go into a domain table later.

# Part 3 — Länder / city counts that Destatis does not publish

Every figure is stored in `metric` under its own `source_id` and its own metric name. Nothing is written to `pit`,
and no Destatis row is touched: the Destatis metric is `housed_homeless*`, while these are `nrw_*` / `be_*`.
All are official counts, so `is_est=0`.

## Sources (archived as `data/raw/counts_law/<key>.pdf` plus the sha1 cache copy)
| key | document | URL | bytes |
|---|---|---|---|
| nrw_wnb2024 | MAGS NRW / IT.NRW, *Integrierte Wohnungsnotfall-Berichterstattung 2024 in NRW* (Stichtag 30.06.2024, 38 pp., published 23.06.2025) | https://mags.nrw/system/files/media/document/file/mags-wnb-nrw-2024.pdf | 1,418,829 |
| be_nds2020 | Abgeordnetenhaus Berlin, Drs. 19/10884, Schriftliche Anfrage "1. und 2. Nacht der Solidarität", answered by SenIAS on 24.02.2022 (25 pp.) | https://pardok.parlament-berlin.de/starweb/adis/citat/VT/19/SchrAnfr/S19-10884.pdf | 83,656 |

There is no 2025 NRW report yet: `mags-wnb-nrw-2025.pdf` returns 404, and the MAGS page still lists the 2024 report
as current (checked 2026-11-20).

## Method
- **NRW Land figures (Tab. 2, Tab. 3):** the `FIGURES` list includes a needle. For Tab. 2 the needle is the full printed
  row, e.g. `2024 106 330 15 840 122 170`, so all three values must sit on the same line of the page. NRW prints
  thousands with a space separator. Needles are matched against whitespace-collapsed page text.
- **NRW Kreise (annex Tab. 11–13):** these are parsed straight from the archived PDF (`pdftotext -layout`). Columns are
  split on runs of 2+ spaces. A row counts only if it has exactly 10 cells (2024…2015) and each cell is either a number
  or `.`, which means *keine Angabe* and produces no row. Unparseable rows and missing Kreise are reported in
  `land_problems`; this run reported none. Kreis names map to AGS5 through `NRW_AGS`, which was built by matching the
  report's names against the Destatis 22971-0080 region labels (53/53 matched, none ambiguous, no AGS invented).
  Regierungsbezirke use their 3-digit AGS (051, 053, 055, 057, 059) with `entity_type='regbez'`.
- **Berlin:** each needle is the full phrase from the answer to question 2 (e.g. `12 in Polizeigewahrsam`).

run left the count unchanged (idempotent). `pit` still holds 2,085 rows, all from Destatis. The source table holds 18 rows.

## Caveats (part 3)
- **The NRW concept is not the Destatis concept.** NRW uses a 30 June reference date, not 31 Jan. It covers housed
  persons plus people known to the freie Träger, including informal and unsheltered cases (1,795 in 2024). It
  includes recognised refugees (70.3% in 2024) and adds late reports: about 8,500 refugees were reported for the first
  time in 2024. The NRW and Destatis series must never be summed or substituted for each other.
- **Kreis sums differ slightly from the Land total** in 2021 (+20), 2023 (+15) and 2024 (−15), because from 2021 the
  report rounds Kreis cells to multiples of 5. Each row stores the number as printed. The selftest allows a drift
  of ±110.
- **Large year-to-year jumps at Kreis level are reporting artefacts**, e.g. Krefeld 1,115 → 30 and Wuppertal 230 → 2,230.
  The jump comes from late or missing reports and is stored exactly as printed.
- **Berlin 2020 was a single-night winter count** of visible rough sleepers plus some facilities. It is not a
  homelessness total, and the organisers themselves call it a lower bound.
- The Berlin answer also contains Bezirk breakdowns. They were not ingested because Berlin Bezirke have no AGS of
  their own; Berlin is Land 11, Kreis 11000, Gemeinde 11000000.

## Verified (part 3)
Five spot checks against the archived PDFs via `pdftotext -layout`:
1. NRW 2024 total 122,170 = Tab. 2 row `2024 106 330 15 840 122 170` (p. 9) = Tab. 11 "Nordrhein-Westfalen" (p. 27).
   The MAGS press release of 23.06.2025 gives the same number.
2. Köln 05315, 2024: 10,230 (Tab. 11, p. 26). The DB row reads 10230.0.
3. Dortmund 05913, 2024: 2,245 (Tab. 11, p. 27). The DB row reads 2245.0.
4. NRW ohne Unterkunft 2024: 1,795, both in the Tab. 3 table and in the body text on p. 10.
5. Berlin 2020 total 1,976 and the six components: Drs. 19/10884 p. 3. The components sum to
   807+15+158+12+942+42 = 1,976 ✓.

# Part 3c — Frankfurt am Main (manual archive)
Source: *Kommunale Wohnraumversorgung – Jahresbericht 2024*, Stadt Frankfurt am Main, Amt für Wohnungswesen.
- URL: https://frankfurt.de/-/media/frankfurtde/service-und-rathaus/verwaltung/aemter-und-institutionen/amt-fuer-wohnungswesen/pdf/64_3/64_3_2024_bericht-kommunale-wohnraumversorgung.pdf
- frankfurt.de returns 403 to the project UA. The Lead archived the file via a browser on 2026-11-21. Archived copy:
  `data/raw/counts_law/ffm_wohnraum2024.pdf`, 29 pages, PDF 1.6, 535,378 bytes, sha256
  7251a2523b6e54cfaf9c608942a0571d3b719c6c868e043719217f0a29ecd264 (checked by me; it matches the Lead's value).
- The source row note reads "manual archive via browser, 2026-11-21". The module never fetches this URL (`DOC_LOCAL`).
- Licence: an amtliche Veröffentlichung of the city, cited with source.

**What it is:** the city's register of households entitled to social housing. Its figures are **registered
households**, not a count of all homeless people. They go to `metric` (entity kreis 06412) with unit `households`,
or `persons` where printed. They never go into `pit`. Each row carries `quote` (the verbatim table line) and
`locator` (PDF page).

| metric | value | unit | page | verbatim line |
|---|---|---|---|---|

Totals across household sizes are not stored, because the report does not print them per row.

# Part 4 — Law (`law`) and court decisions (`doc`, kind `court`)

`ingest_law()` fetches each source with the common fetcher (project user agent, 1 req/s) and archives it as
`data/raw/counts_law/law_<key>.pdf|html` plus the sha1 cache copy. A row is written **only if every listed verbatim
phrase is found** in the source text: whitespace-collapsed, read in both pdftotext layout and reading order, with
hyphenation removed. Summaries paraphrase only those phrases. The first phrase is stored in `summary` as
`[verified phrase: „…“]`. Disable with `--no-law`.

Results: **25 law rows (incl. 8 plans) + 7 court docs** (Bremen from the Lead's manual archive; SH from a qpdf excerpt of the 1992 gazette volume). A second run gives the
same counts (idempotent). A law row of this domain whose (jurisdiction, citation, title) is no longer listed in
`LAW` is pruned on the next run. This happened once: `§ 11 Nds. SOG (Neubekanntmachung 2005)` was replaced by
`§ 11 NPOG`.

## Versions (Lead decision 2026-11-21)
Every law row has a version entry in `LAW_META`, which is written to the domain table `counts_law_law_meta`
(`law_id, law_key, as_of, as_of_text, text_kind, note, local_file, ocr, ocr_tool, needs_spot_check, version_url`).
The same information is prefixed to `law.summary` as `[consolidated text|original promulgated text|policy document;
as of …; note]`. `as_of_text` is a verbatim string that must occur in the archived **version source**: the row's own
source, or `version_url` where a single-norm page shows no Stand. If it is missing, the row is not written.

**Berlin OCR:** OCR was not needed. The berlin.de `asog.pdf` was OCR-checked (tesseract 5.5.3, `deu`). It turned out
to be the *Ausführungsvorschriften zu § 49 ASOG (Dateienrichtlinien)* from Amtsblatt 2013, not the act itself. The
official GVBl. Berlin 2006 Nr. 34 PDF on berlin.de has a text layer, so § 17 is verified against pdftotext. All rows
have `ocr=0, needs_spot_check=0`. The OCR path stays in the schema (`ocr_tool`, `needs_spot_check`) for later scans.

Caveats:
- BW, NI, HE, MV and BE are **original promulgated texts**: GBl., GVBl., Neubekanntmachung, or a ministry print.
  Later amendments are not verified, and the flag says so in `summary` and `counts_law_law_meta`. A general
  clause is rarely amended, but that has not been checked against an archived text.
- MV: the archived text is the Ministerium für Inneres und Europa brochure on the Land's own portal (land-mv.de),
  not the GVOBl. issue itself (GVOBl. M-V 2020 S. 334). It states which law it reproduces.
- The NRW "Endlich ein ZUHAUSE!" page also lists contact persons at welfare organisations. Only the page is archived;
  no names are stored in any table.

| VG Köln, Beschl. v. 01.08.2024 – 22 L 1420/24 | NRWE | ECLI:DE:VGK:2024:0801.22L1420.24.00 | No claim to a specific accommodation (relief refused) |
| VG Arnsberg, Beschl. v. 19.06.2019 – 6 L 898/19 | NRWE | ECLI:DE:VGAR:2019:0619.6L898.19.00 | Involuntarily homeless persons have a claim to full-day humane accommodation by the municipality where they actually stay |
| BVerwG, Urt. v. 18.02.2021 – 1 C 4.20 | bverwg.de | ECLI:DE:BVerwG:2021:180221U1C4.20.0 | Asylum law (Art. 3 ECHR standard): no flat of one's own is needed if access to changing accommodation avoids homelessness with sufficient certainty. A related standard, not a municipal duty-to-house case. |
| BVerwG, Urt. v. 21.02.2019 – 4 C 9.18 | bverwg.de | ECLI:DE:BVerwG:2019:210219U4C9.18.0 | § 246 Abs. 9 BauGB covers only projects through which the public sector exercises its accommodation responsibility for refugees. Related, not homelessness law proper. |
| BayVGH, Beschl. v. 15.02.2024 – 4 CE 24.60 | gesetze-bayern.de (PDF, 5 pp.) | header "VGH München, Beschluss v. 15.02.2024 – 4 CE 24.60" (no ECLI printed) | Family reunification without the housing requirement does not release the municipality from its Art. 6, 7 Abs. 2 Nr. 3 LStVG duty; the municipality of stay is competent (Gründe Rn. 10, 14) |
| BayVGH, Beschl. v. 30.04.2024 – 4 CE 24.627 | gesetze-bayern.de (PDF) | header "VGH München, Beschluss v. 30.04.2024 – 4 CE 24.627" | Local competence follows the current place of stay where the danger arises, not where homelessness first occurred (Gründe Rn. 10) |

The decisions are anonymised at the source. Only the citation, court, date, Land and our own paraphrase are stored.
The texts are archived under `data/raw/counts_law/law_<key>.*` (gitignored). For the BayVGH rows, needles and
summaries come **only from the Gründe**: per the portal's Nutzungshinweise the editorial Leitsätze (BAYERN.RECHT /
C.H.Beck) are not free under § 5 UrhG. Each source row's `license` field records this.

funding programme for building, not a homelessness strategy, so it is not ingested.

# Part 5 — Municipal law (`law`, kind satzung/verordnung) and shelter fees (`metric`)

Lead request 2026-11-21 (laws parity). One `law` row per municipal document: `jurisdiction` = `jurisdiction_ags` =
8-digit Gemeinde AGS of the issuing city (schema 3; for a kreisfreie Stadt the Kreis AGS + `000`, for a
kreisangehörige city its own GV-ISys AGS), `kind` satzung or verordnung, `topic` from obdach | obdach_gebuehr |
betteln | lagern | alkohol | sondernutzung. A row is written only if every needle (title / identifying sentence, the
version text, and each fee quote) is found verbatim in the archived text (pdftotext layout + reading order or
stripped HTML; whitespace collapsed, soft hyphens removed). Version per row in `counts_law_law_meta` (as_of,
as_of_text, text_kind). Fees go to `metric` (entity_type `gemeinde`, the city's Gemeinde AGS) with `einheit`,
`quote` (the verbatim line) and `locator` (§ / annex). Licence for all municipal sources:
*Amtliches Werk (§ 5 Abs. 1 UrhG): kommunale Satzung/Verordnung, amtlich bekanntgemacht; zitiert mit Fundstelle*. Since 2026-11-23 the module itself reads robots.txt per host before the first network request
(project UA, longest-match rules, fail closed: a disallowed path or an unreadable robots.txt skips the host and is
logged; Crawl-delay honoured, never < 1 s). A re-audit with these rules found 4 earlier sources (Darmstadt ×2,
Landshut ×2) whose paths robots.txt disallows; batch 18 withdrew them, and since 2026-11-23 they are read only from

muni_landshut_sondernutzung.pdf, muni_landshut_obdach.pdf; never fetched). No host sent a `tdm-reservation` header.

### Hosts the robots check would skip without the raw cache (for the Lead to archive by hand if a refresh is needed)

fetch() reads data/raw/counts_law/ first; a cache hit needs no request and no robots check. On a forced refresh these
hosts would fail closed (robots.txt unreadable) and the rows would be skipped, not pruned. Seen 2026-11-23, not retried.

### Lapse test for police / public-order ordinances from 2006 or earlier (Lead ruling 2026-11-23, b19)

Rule: keep a row only if its text is a re-enactment/Neufassung, carries its own lapse clause, or has an end date after
today (2026-11-23); an amendment alone does not restart the clock (NW § 32 Abs. 1 OBG: 20 years, Abs. 2 amendments
excluded; BW § 32 PolG: 20 years; BY Art. 50 LStVG: 20 years; HE § 79 HSOG: 30 years; NI § 61 NPOG: 20 years).
The deciding sentence is a needle of the row (G9).

| row | Land | base | deciding sentence (needle) | decision |

Januar 2005“ (29.09.2025 is an amending ordinance) | **dropped** — not in force (2005 base lapsed 2025); the row was only in b18b (8952bea, not ingested), so it is simply removed |
| herne_strassenvo | NW | OBVO 21.12.2005, published 02.01.2006 | „Die öffentliche Bekanntmachung der Ordnungsbehördlichen Straßenverordnung erfolgte in den Herner Ausgaben der WAZ am 02.01.2006“; no own lapse clause, later changes are amendments | **lapsed 03.01.2026** — kept as a historical row with status 'lapsed' (Lead, INGEST_CONTRACT §11); (b) counts as lapsed, not covered |
| hagen_gebietsordnung | NW | Gebietsordnung 24.10.1985 | „Öffentlich bekannt gemacht am 29. Oktober 1985, in Kraft seit dem 6. November 1985“; I.–VII. Nachtrag are amendments | **lapsed 06.11.2005** — kept as a historical row with status 'lapsed' (Lead, §11); (b) counts as lapsed, not covered |

Zuwiderhandlungen gegen § 3 dieser Verordnung, insbesondere unerlaubtes Lagern, Nächtigen, Betteln und Niederlassen zum Alkoholgenuss, nach Art. 23, 24 Abs. 1 Nr. 1, Abs. 2 Satz 2 Gemeindeordnung“ vs. „5. … § 5 … nach Art. 16 Abs. 2 Landesstraf- und Verordnungsgesetz“ / „6. … § 6 … nach Art. 28 Abs. 2 Landesstraf- und Verordnungsgesetz“ | **b20 section check: in force (reverses the b19/Lead lapsed status).** The row's content, § 3 (no Sondernutzungserlaubnis for Lagern/Nächtigen, Alkoholgenuss, Betteln), rests on Art. 23, 24 GO (Satzung law; fine under § 7 Nr. 3), so Art. 50 LStVG does not apply (Art. 50 Abs. 3); only the LStVG-based §§ 5 (pigeon feeding) and 6 (posters) lapsed on 01.11.2016. Hildesheim 60.03 Sondernutzungssatzung (in force 22.02.2024) is a Satzung, not time-limited, and contains no begging, lagern or alcohol rule, so no (b) row; Hildesheim (b) is covered by 10.01 (alcohol on playgrounds only).
Leipzig (2020) and Mannheim (2021) police ordinances have no year in their citation but are 2020/2021 enactments; not affected.

### Fixed-term ordinances (b20): `in_force_to` from the rule's own end clause, status stays in_force

Convention (same as lapsed_on, INGEST_CONTRACT §11): in_force_to is the first day the rule is no longer in force —
„gilt bis (zum Ablauf des) X“ / „mit Ablauf des X außer Kraft“ / „befristet bis X“ → X + 1 day; „tritt am X außer Kraft“ → X.
The end clause is a needle of the row (or, for Hamburg Hbf, the amendment's as_of_text), verified in the archived text.
Relative terms („N Jahre nach Inkrafttreten“) are filled only where the same clause states the entry date (Magdeburg);
statutory maximum terms (§ 32 OBG NRW, Art. 50 LStVG, § 61 NPOG …) are not written as in_force_to.

BGH decisions are only served through juris, which we never scrape. BSG B 8 SO 22/10 R (mentioned in part 4) was not looked up in this pass.

## Verify notes (part 5)
`scripts/verify.py --db <scratch>` passes G1, G2, G4–G8 and G10. Two gates fail for reasons outside this module:
- **G3**: the municipal source URLs are not yet in `docs/SOURCES.md` (that file belongs to the Lead). The rows to add are in the "SOURCES.md rows" block above.
- **G9**: integer fees (e.g. Bochum 370, Mannheim 16/21, Augsburg 330, Freiburg 10/600/420, Braunschweig 10/9) are printed as "370,00 €". `forms()` builds only "370" / "1.234" for an integer value, so the token is not found. Suggested fix (Lead's file): for an integer value i, also yield `f"{i},00"` and the grouped form plus ",00". The G5 numeric check finds the same values.


## Schema 3 (rebase onto 70e3e9d)

- Municipal statutes/ordinances (`law.jurisdiction` = `law.jurisdiction_ags`) and municipal fee metrics
  (`metric.entity_type='gemeinde'`) now carry the 8-digit Gemeinde AGS of the issuing city. All 36 cities are
  kreisfreie Städte, whose Gemeinde AGS is the Kreis AGS + `000` (e.g. Köln 05315000, Hamburg 02000000);
  `_mu(..., gem=...)` takes an explicit AGS for kreisangehörige cities. At run time each AGS is checked
  against the Lead's `geo_gemeinde` (exists, name matches); a mismatch skips the row with a problem note.
  When `geo_gemeinde` is empty the run notes `muni_gemeinde_unchecked=<n>`.
- Old Kreis-level municipal rows written by earlier versions are removed on re-ingest (own rows only):
  law rows via the existing prune of unlisted (jurisdiction, citation, title), fee metrics via
  `muni_kreis_fee_rows_moved`. Tested on a copy of the schema-2 scratch DB: 52 law rows re-keyed, 76 fee rows moved.
- The module writes no `geo_*` rows any more (the former `INSERT OR IGNORE` into `geo_land`/`geo_kreis` from
  GENESIS labels is gone). Destatis region codes missing from the Lead's geo are only reported (`geo_absent`).
- All metric writes go through `common.upsert` with AGS entity ids (Land `05`/`11`/`02`/`08`/`09`, Kreis 5-digit,
  Gemeinde 8-digit); the only raw SQL on `metric` is the Eisenach fold `UPDATE` of value/anmerkung, which does not
  touch `entity_id`. The selftest asserts that every metric row of this domain has a valid AGS for its level.
- Scratch-only runs now open the `--db` file directly via `run.open_db(path)` (no change of `common.DB`, no
  `data/atlas.lock`), as required by the 70e3e9d direct-run guard.

## Licence audit (2026-11-21)

- Municipal Satzungen and Polizei-/Ordnungsverordnungen: § 5 Abs. 1 UrhG applies (Satzungen as Gesetze im
  materiellen Sinn, Verordnungen named in the provision); kept as `LIC_MUNI`. Publisher names now use the official
  Stadt designation (Landeshauptstadt …, Bundesstadt Bonn, Wissenschaftsstadt Darmstadt, Klingenstadt Solingen).
- Plans and reports are not Gesetze/Verordnungen/Erlasse, so the former blanket "Amtliches Werk, gemeinfrei nach
  § 5 Abs. 1 UrhG" was wrong for them. Each plan source now carries its own terms (`PLAN_LIC`): Parlamentsdrucksachen
  (HH 21/16901, SH 20/4332, HB Mitteilung des Senats 23.04.2024) as § 5 Abs. 2 UrhG works with the host's stated
  terms; RP and TH under the ministries' Impressum terms (copyright reserved, so title, date, own summary and a short
  quote only); the SN Gemeinsame Empfehlungen are a Verwaltungsvorschrift (§ 5 Abs. 1 UrhG). Other plan rows default to
  `LIC_PLAN`.
- Already in docs/SOURCES.md and inaccurate (append-only file, so for the Lead to correct): rows for the NAP 2024
  (BMWSB), Berlin Leitlinien 2026, NRW "Endlich ein ZUHAUSE!" and the NRW Housing First Umsetzungskonzept say
  "Amtliches Werk, gemeinfrei nach § 5 Abs. 1 UrhG". Suggested text: `LIC_PLAN` ("Amtliche Veröffentlichung
  (Plan/Bericht), kein Gesetz oder Verordnung, daher nicht § 5 Abs. 1 UrhG; im Atlas nur Titel, Datum, eigene
  Zusammenfassung und kurzes Zitat mit Quellenangabe (§ 51 UrhG), kein Volltext"). The module now writes that text
  into `source.license` for those four sources.
- G3 proof: the 60 rows above appended to a scratch copy of docs/SOURCES.md (scratch/SOURCES_g3test.md, not
